- **Human review:** **Review required:** Confirm that inline single-line block comments after `else` should stay immediately before its block or nested `if`, including recursively formatted `if` expressions and `let … else`, while comments containing line breaks remain unsupported.
- **Remaining scope:** Support comments on other branch and match-arm tokens, then continue through remaining comment-bearing source syntax.

### IR-414: Preserve comments after match-arm arrows

- **Decision:** Preserve single-line block comments attached to the `FAT_ARROW` token of a match arm between `=>` and its body. Support guarded arms, block-like bodies, and match expressions recursively formatted inside another expression. Keep line comments, multiline block comments, and comments attached to other match-arm tokens fail-closed.
- **Reason:** The arrow directly introduces the arm's right-hand side, and a single-line block comment can stay in that gap without changing the arm's meaning. Appending it before the existing right-hand-side layout keeps the comment with `=>` while preserving the documented flat, broken, and block-like body choices.
- **Review:** Regressions cover unguarded and guarded arms, a block body that forces the arm list to break, a nested match expression, canonical spacing, and fixed-point output. Parsed refusal cases cover line comments and multiline block comments. Hostile review found no actionable issues. All 347 `sai_fmt` tests and the full workspace tests, including doc tests, pass; workspace Clippy with warnings denied, `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, and `git diff --check` pass.
- **Human review:** **Review required:** Confirm that inline single-line block comments after a match-arm `=>` should stay immediately before the body, including guarded and recursively formatted arms, while line-breaking comments and comments on other arm tokens remain unsupported.
- **Remaining scope:** Support comments on match-arm pattern tokens and guard expressions, then continue through remaining comment-bearing source syntax.

### IR-415: Preserve comments after match-guard `if` keywords

- **Decision:** Preserve single-line block comments attached to the `IF_KW` token of a `MATCH_GUARD` between `if` and its guard expression, including in recursively formatted match expressions. Keep line comments, multiline block comments, and comments attached to guard-expression interiors or pattern interiors fail-closed; final-token pattern comments are covered by IR-416.
- **Reason:** The guard's direct `if` token introduces the expression that follows it. Keeping a single-line block comment in that gap preserves the guard association and leaves the arrow and right-hand-side layout unchanged. Selecting the token from `MATCH_GUARD` avoids confusing it with an `if` expression elsewhere in the arm.
- **Review:** Regressions cover guard expressions in direct and nested match expressions, canonical spacing, fixed-point output, line and multiline comment refusal, and fail-closed behavior for a comment inside a constructor pattern. Hostile review confirmed that `MATCH_GUARD.IF_KW` and `MATCH_ARM.FAT_ARROW` are tracked independently and that comments appear in their token order; no actionable findings remain. All 349 `sai_fmt` tests and the full workspace tests, including doc tests, pass; workspace Clippy with warnings denied, `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, and `git diff --check` pass.
- **Human review:** **Review required:** Confirm that a single-line block comment after a match guard's `if` stays before the guard expression, while line-breaking comments and comments on pattern or guard-expression interiors remain unsupported.
- **Remaining scope:** Support comments inside match guard expressions and other nested pattern tokens, then continue through remaining comment-bearing source syntax.

### IR-416: Preserve comments after match-arm patterns

- **Decision:** Preserve single-line block comments attached to the final token of a match-arm pattern, placing them after the formatted pattern and before its optional guard or `=>`. Support simple and composite patterns, including patterns in recursively formatted match expressions. Keep comments attached to tokens inside a pattern fail-closed.
- **Reason:** A trailing comment belongs to the pattern's final token, whether that token closes a constructor or is part of a simple pattern. Reading trivia from the AST pattern's actual last token keeps the comment after the complete pattern and before any guard or arm arrow without guessing from pattern kind.
- **Review:** Regressions cover simple member and nested constructor patterns, a combined pattern/guard/arrow comment sequence, a nested match argument, canonical spacing, and fixed-point output. A parser-valid comment inside a constructor pattern remains fail-closed. Hostile review found no actionable issues and confirmed that pattern, guard `if`, and arm arrow trivia retain their token order. All 351 `sai_fmt` tests and the full workspace tests, including doc tests, pass; workspace Clippy with warnings denied, `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, and `git diff --check` pass.
- **Human review:** **Review required:** Confirm that a single-line block comment on the final token of a match pattern stays after the complete pattern and before its optional guard or arrow, while comments inside the pattern remain unsupported.
- **Remaining scope:** Support comments inside match guard expressions and other nested pattern tokens, then continue through remaining comment-bearing source syntax.

### IR-417: Preserve comments after match-guard expressions

- **Decision:** Preserve single-line block comments attached to the final token of a match guard's expression after the formatted expression and before the arm's `=>`, including call expressions and guards in nested match expressions. Keep comments attached to interior expression tokens fail-closed.
- **Reason:** The trailing comment belongs to the guard expression's actual final token. Collecting trivia from that token and appending it after the expression document keeps it between the guard and arm arrow without confusing it with comments after `if` or `=>`.
- **Review:** Regressions cover call-expression guards in direct and nested match expressions, a combined pattern/guard-keyword/guard-expression/arrow comment sequence, canonical spacing, and fixed-point output. A parser-valid comment inside a guard call argument remains fail-closed. Hostile review found no actionable issues and confirmed independent ownership and source order for all four token positions. All 353 `sai_fmt` tests and the full workspace tests, including doc tests, pass; workspace Clippy with warnings denied, `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, and `git diff --check` pass.
- **Human review:** **Review required:** Confirm that a single-line block comment on the final token of a match guard expression stays after the full expression and before `=>`, while comments on interior expression tokens remain unsupported.
- **Remaining scope:** Support comments attached to match-arm result expressions and separators, then continue through remaining comment-bearing source syntax.

### IR-418: Preserve comments after match-arm result expressions

- **Decision:** Preserve single-line block comments attached to the final token of a match arm's result expression after the formatted result, including block-like results and expressions in nested match expressions. Keep line comments, multiline block comments, and comments attached to interior result-expression tokens fail-closed.
- **Reason:** The trailing comment belongs to the result expression's actual final token. Collecting trivia from that token and appending it after the complete result document keeps it before the arm separator while ensuring conditional parentheses and block layout remain inside the comment's scope.
- **Review:** Regressions cover a literal result, a block-like result in a nested match, canonical spacing, fixed-point output, parser-valid line and multiline comment refusals, and a parser-valid comment inside a result call. Hostile review found no actionable issues. All 356 `sai_fmt` tests and the full workspace tests, including doc tests, pass; workspace Clippy with warnings denied, `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, and `git diff --check` pass.
- **Human review:** **Review required:** Confirm that a single-line block comment on the final token of a match arm's result expression stays after the complete result and before its separator, while line-breaking comments and result-expression interior comments remain unsupported.
- **Remaining scope:** Support comments before match arms, then continue through remaining comment-bearing source syntax.

### IR-419: Preserve comments after match-arm separators

- **Decision:** Preserve single-line block comments attached to a comma between match arms after the comma and before the following arm. Keep line comments, multiline block comments, comments after a trailing comma, and comments on other match-arm list tokens fail-closed.
- **Reason:** A comment in the comma's trailing trivia belongs to that separator. Carrying it as a suffix for the preceding arm lets flat and broken list layouts preserve both the comma and its comment without changing separators that contain no comment.
- **Review:** Regressions cover comments between arms in flat and forced-broken lists, canonical spacing, fixed-point output, parser-valid line and multiline comment refusals, and fail-closed behavior after a trailing comma. Hostile review found no actionable issues and confirmed that lists without separator comments retain their previous flat and broken behavior. All 359 `sai_fmt` tests and the full workspace tests, including doc tests, pass; workspace Clippy with warnings denied, `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, and `git diff --check` pass.
- **Human review:** **Review required:** Confirm that a single-line block comment attached to an arm-separating comma stays after that comma in both flat and broken layouts, while comments after a trailing comma remain unsupported.
- **Remaining scope:** Support comments before match arms and after trailing commas, then continue through remaining comment-bearing source syntax.

### IR-420: Preserve comments after trailing match-arm commas

- **Decision:** Preserve single-line block comments attached to a trailing comma after the final match arm before the closing brace, including single-arm lists and nested match expressions whose layout is forced to break. Keep line comments and multiline block comments fail-closed.
- **Reason:** The comment belongs to the final arm-list separator. A suffix slot after the last arm keeps the comma and comment together before the closing brace in both flat and broken layouts.
- **Review:** Regressions cover a single-arm flat match, a nested broken match with a block result, fixed-point output, and parser-valid line and multiline comment refusals after both inter-arm and trailing commas. Hostile review found no actionable issues and confirmed that the final suffix is rendered in both layouts while empty lists retain the previous output. All 359 `sai_fmt` tests and the full workspace tests, including doc tests, pass; workspace Clippy with warnings denied, `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, and `git diff --check` pass.
- **Human review:** **Review required:** Confirm that a single-line block comment after a trailing match-arm comma stays after the comma and before the closing brace in flat and broken lists, while line-breaking comments remain unsupported.
- **Remaining scope:** Support comments before match arms, then continue through remaining comment-bearing source syntax.

### IR-421: Preserve comments before match-arm patterns

- **Decision:** Preserve single-line block comments in the trailing trivia of a non-empty match-arm list's opening brace and in the leading trivia of an arm pattern's first token. Place them before the pattern, including for newline-separated arms and recursively formatted match expressions. Keep line comments, multiline block comments, and comments on pattern-interior tokens fail-closed.
- **Reason:** The parser can assign a comment before the first pattern to either the opening brace or the pattern's first token. Render both ownership positions in the same pre-pattern position so reparsing does not change the formatter's next output.
- **Review:** Regressions cover opening-brace and pattern-leading comments, newline-separated arms, nested broken match layout, fixed-point output, and parser-valid line and multiline comment refusals. Hostile review confirmed that the fixed-point issue from the first draft is resolved and found no other actionable findings. All 361 `sai_fmt` tests and the full workspace tests, including doc tests, pass; workspace Clippy with warnings denied, `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, and `git diff --check` pass.
- **Human review:** **Review required:** Confirm that a single-line block comment attached to the opening brace or first token of a match-arm pattern stays immediately before the pattern, while line-breaking comments and pattern-interior comments remain unsupported.
- **Remaining scope:** Support comments before closing match-arm lists and other remaining source tokens, then continue through remaining comment-bearing syntax.

### IR-422: Preserve comments before closing match-arm lists

- **Decision:** Preserve comments attached to the leading trivia of a match-arm list's closing brace immediately before that brace. Support empty and non-empty lists, including nested match expressions, line comments, multiline block comments, and blank-line gaps between the final arm and the comments.
- **Reason:** The parser assigns these comments to the `R_BRACE`, rather than to the final arm or its separator. Rendering them as a closing-list suffix keeps their ownership and order while forcing a broken layout; comment-only lists also need a broken form.
- **Review:** Regressions cover comments after a final result comment, after a commented trailing comma, before empty and non-empty list closes, and inside a nested match. They verify preserved blank lines between the last arm and closing comments and fixed-point output. Hostile review questioned removing a blank line after the opening brace of a comment-only list; formatter spec §14.6.4 rule 2 requires removing blank lines at list edges, so the case now verifies that normalization. All 362 `sai_fmt` tests and the full workspace test suite, including doc tests, pass; workspace Clippy with warnings denied, `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, and `git diff --check` pass.
- **Human review:** **Review required:** Confirm that comments attached to a match-arm list's closing brace should print one indentation level inside the list, including line and multiline comments, and that blank lines are preserved between the final arm and those comments but not at the list edges.
- **Remaining scope:** Continue through remaining comment-bearing source syntax and other unsupported formatter shapes.

### IR-423: Preserve comments before match-arm list opening braces

- **Decision:** Preserve own-line comments attached to the leading trivia of a match-arm list's opening brace immediately before that brace. Support line and multiline block comments, empty and non-empty lists, nested matches, and blank-line gaps between consecutive comments and before the brace.
- **Reason:** The parser attaches these comments to the `L_BRACE`, distinct from comments trailing the opening brace that belong before the first pattern. Rendering the leading comments before `{` preserves their token ownership and gives them the brace's indentation.
- **Review:** Regressions cover line comments, multiline block comments, multiple comments with blank-line gaps, empty lists, nested expressions, and fixed-point output. Hostile review found no ownership, placement, indentation, blank-line, or idempotency issue; it caught a stale general support statement, which was narrowed to exclude this newly documented `MATCH_ARM_LIST` exception and confirmed as resolved. All 363 `sai_fmt` tests and the full workspace test suite, including doc tests, pass; workspace Clippy with warnings denied, `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, and `git diff --check` pass.
- **Human review:** **Review required:** Confirm that an own-line comment attached to a match-arm list's opening brace should appear before `{` at the brace's indentation, while own-line comments before other non-block opening braces remain unsupported.
- **Remaining scope:** Continue through remaining match-expression comment positions and other unsupported formatter syntax.

### IR-424: Preserve comments after match scrutinees

- **Decision:** Preserve trailing comments on the final token of a match scrutinee between the formatted scrutinee and its arm-list opening brace. Keep a single-line block comment inline; force a line break after a trailing line comment or multiline block comment. Keep comments on interior scrutinee tokens fail-closed.
- **Reason:** The scrutinee may be any supported expression, so selecting its actual final token preserves comment ownership without restricting the rule to a particular expression shape. Rendering the trailing comments before the arm list follows the token order and the formatter's trailing-comment line-break rules.
- **Review:** Regressions cover simple and call scrutinees, line and multiline block comments, a nested match argument, interaction with an own-line comment before `{`, interior-comment refusal, and fixed-point output. Hostile review caught a blank-line preservation bug between a trailing scrutinee comment and the first opening-brace comment; the output now removes that blank line as required inside an expression while retaining gaps within the own-line comment group. Re-review found no further issue. All 365 `sai_fmt` tests and the full workspace test suite, including doc tests, pass; workspace Clippy with warnings denied, `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, and `git diff --check` pass.
- **Human review:** **Review required:** Confirm that a trailing line comment or multiline block comment on a match scrutinee should move the opening brace to the next line, while a single-line block comment stays inline and comments on interior scrutinee tokens remain unsupported.
- **Remaining scope:** Continue through remaining comment-bearing source syntax and unsupported formatter shapes.

### IR-425: Preserve line comments after match keywords

- **Decision:** Preserve trailing comments attached to `MATCH_KW` for match statements and match expressions used directly as `let`/`var` initializers or assignment right-hand sides. Keep a single-line block comment inline; place the scrutinee on the next line after a line comment or multiline block comment. Keep match-keyword comments nested inside composite expressions fail-closed.
- **Reason:** The keyword directly introduces the scrutinee. Emitting its trailing comments with the common trailing-comment rules preserves that order and forces the next line only when the comment requires it, without broadening comment ownership for recursively nested match expressions.
- **Review:** Regressions cover line and multiline comments in statement, initializer, and assignment contexts, fixed-point output, and parsed fail-closed cases for nested composite expressions. Hostile review found no layout or ownership defect; it caught a stale support statement, which was updated to reflect the direct-context support. All 365 `sai_fmt` tests and the full workspace test suite, including doc tests, pass; workspace Clippy with warnings denied, `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, and `git diff --check` pass.
- **Human review:** **Review required:** Confirm that line and multiline block comments attached to `MATCH_KW` should move the scrutinee to the next line in direct statement and root initializer/assignment contexts, while nested composite contexts remain fail-closed.
- **Remaining scope:** Continue through remaining comment-bearing source syntax and unsupported formatter shapes.

### IR-426: Preserve line comments after match-arm arrows

- **Decision:** Preserve line comments and multiline block comments attached to a match arm's `FAT_ARROW`. Keep line comments at the end of the arrow line and place the body on an indented continuation line; preserve multiline block-comment text before that continuation. Continue supporting single-line block comments inline.
- **Reason:** The arrow introduces the body, and a comment that forces the body onto a later line must make the arm list break. Indenting the body continuation keeps it subordinate to the arm while retaining the existing parenthesization rule for open-ended range bodies in broken lists.
- **Review:** Regressions cover line and multiline comments, mixed multiline and single-line block-comment runs, block-like bodies, nested matches, open-ended range results, and fixed-point output. The first hostile review found that the generic trailing-comment renderer could put a hard line between two arrow comments, reattaching the second comment as body-leading trivia and breaking idempotency. A match-arrow-specific renderer keeps block-comment runs attached to the arrow while line comments still force separation. Re-review found no actionable issues and checked block-then-line comments, consecutive multiline block comments, inline block-plus-line comments, and initializer/assignment RHS contexts; all 16 match-arm tests passed.
- **Human review:** **Review required:** Confirm that a line or multiline block comment attached to `=>` should remain on the arrow line and move the body to an indented continuation, including block-like and nested results.
- **Remaining scope:** Continue through remaining match-arm tokens and other unsupported comment-bearing syntax.

### IR-427: Preserve line comments after match-guard `if`

- **Decision:** Preserve line comments and multiline block comments attached to a match guard's `IF_KW`. Keep the comment after `if` and move the guard expression to an indented continuation line; keep single-line block comments inline. Use the same comment-run rendering rule as match-arm arrow comments so following block comments remain attached across formatting passes.
- **Reason:** The guard keyword introduces its condition. A comment that extends across lines should not absorb or visually join the condition, and an indented continuation makes the relationship to the arm clear.
- **Review:** Focused regressions cover line and multiline comments, mixed multiline and single-line block-comment runs, nested matches, and fixed-point output. Hostile review found no runtime or formatting issues; it flagged the implementation guide's stale unsupported-status sentence, which was updated to describe the continuation layout and confirmed.
- **Human review:** **Review required:** Confirm that comments after a guard's `if` stay on that line and the condition moves to an indented continuation, including nested matches and mixed block-comment runs.
- **Remaining scope:** Match-arm pattern and guard-expression interior comments, plus remaining unsupported comment-bearing syntax.
